# LITE: can the EPIC loss be prevented, and at what cost? Protection study (2026-10-15)

Research only. No engine, config, template or test file was changed. Nothing was committed, and the bot API was not called. Cache-only:
no Binance call was made.
- The grid was pre-registered in `reports/LITE_PROTECT_PREREG.txt` (frozen 18:40:45 UTC) before any cell was priced.
- The pre-registration includes the operator's addition: "move the stop to 2 % / 1.5 % / 1 % after +2 %".
- 33 rules were tested, each under 3 price rulers.
- This study has not been reviewed, so it supports no arm and no ship.

## Plain-English summary

**Short answer: EPIC itself could have been saved. Every rule that saves it also costs more on the winners it cuts than it earns on the losers it rescues. No rule passes.**

**What EPIC needed.** EPIC went to +2.60 and then fell to −3.43. On its 1m bars, every lock or trailing rule tested would have booked +0.5 to +1.6 instead (about +0.3 to +1.4 after live slippage). That is roughly 4 to 5 points better than the live −3.43. A lower target of +2.5 would have booked +2.5.

**DUSK could not be saved by any of these rules.** It peaked at only +1.24 (1m bars) before its stop, so no rule armed in time (Δ 0 on every rule).

**How big is the "EPIC problem"?** These numbers use today's exit with the resting +3 limit.
- **2026 (624 backtest trades).**
  - 402 trades reached +2. Of those, 336 (84 %) went on to fill +3 and 64 (10 % of all trades) fell back to −3. Almost none ended at the 12 h cap.
  - Turning those 64 losses into 0 would add +0.31 %/trade to the whole sleeve.
  - At +2.5: 361 reached it, 93 % went on to +3, and 25 fell to −3 (worth +0.12 %/trade).
- **2025.** 438 of 828 reached +2. 77 % went on to +3 and 92 fell to −3 (worth +0.33 %/trade).
- **Once a trade reaches +2 and does not fill +3, it ends at −3 93–97 % of the time.** So an EPIC-type loss is common: about 1 trade in 10.
- **The catch:** for every EPIC there are about 5 trades that touch +2, dip back, and still reach +3.

**Why no rule pays (two-sided, real trade prices).** Example: "after +2, move the stop to +1.5" (P1_A2_L1.5), resting-limit ruler.
- **2026:** it saves 65 losers (+290 points) and cuts 181 winners (−280 points), for +0.016 %/trade (P 0.60).
  - With live slippage on the lock exit it turns negative (−0.06).
  - On the 20 real master trades it is −0.43 %/trade.
- **2025:** it looks better (+0.10). That gain lives entirely in the 2025 trades priced on 1-minute bars. On the 2025 trades priced on tick prints it is −0.13.

**Same story for every family:**
- **Near-target lock (P1), 9 cells:** 2026 −0.09 to +0.02.
- **Trailing stop after +2 / +2.5 (P2), 4 cells:** 2026 −0.04 to +0.005.
- **Scale-out, part sold at +1.5 / +2 (P3), 8 cells:** 2026 −0.16 to −0.01. The break-even versions are the worst.
- **Lower resting target +2 / +2.25 / +2.5 (P4), 3 cells:** 2026 −0.07 to −0.02. Master −0.30 to 0.

**The operator's "move the stop after +2 %" (own section below):**
- **Stop to +1 / +1.5 / +2:** in 2026 these come out at −0.018, +0.016 and −0.064 (resting-limit ruler), and −0.06 to −0.19 with live slippage. Master −0.30 to −0.45.
- **Stop to −1 / −1.5 / −2 (only cut the loss):** about zero to −0.05 in both years; master −0.20 to −0.50.
- **Arming at +1.5 instead:** clearly worse in 2026, −0.13 / −0.17 for the +1 / +1.5 locks.

**Is this a regime switch?** The pattern is consistent. Protection helps in 2025, LITE's losing year, but only on the 1-minute-bar trades. It hurts or is flat in 2026 and on all tick-priced trades.
- The regime read (25 BTC / breadth variables at sign granularity, day units) found no more flags than a day-shuffled null (null ≥ observed 0.62 and 0.38).
- So there is no regime switch to use. This is a hypothesis only, and it is not supported.

**Gates.** Candidates under the zero-slip, resting-limit and live-slip rulers: none.
- No cell has P ≥ 0.90 in both years under any ruler. That includes the EPIC-slip sensitivity (every −3 stop booked at −3.43).
- 31 of 33 cells fail G5: the 2025 tick-priced subset has the opposite sign to the 2025 total.
- All 33 have a negative or zero master Δ.
- About 0.3 false passes per ruler were expected by chance.

**Recommendation (operator decides).**
- Keep LITE at +3 resting / −3 / 12 h. No observe-only Scout line is proposed, because no cell earned a frozen gate.
- **The honest framing:** EPIC-type losses are about 10 % of LITE trades and they cost real money. But they look identical to the about 5× more trades that dip from +2 and still finish at +3, and every rule tested here pays for each rescued EPIC with five trimmed winners.
- **If the operator still wants a protective rule as a declared exception,** the least-bad by the data is "after +2, stop to +1.5" (P1_A2_L1.5): RM +0.10 / +0.016, live-slip +0.03 / −0.06.
  - Arm it with a pre-committed revert gate: revert at the first 15 LITE fills that reach +2 if the realized two-sided Δ (lock exits vs what +3/−3 would have booked, from the shadow) is < 0, or at any 10 consecutive lock exits with ≤ 3 that would have stopped at −3.
  - Expect it to cost about 0.05 %/trade in a 2026-like market. It is insurance, not an edge.

## Candidate list

**None.** G1 in both years: S0 0 · RM 0 · SL 0 · SLp 0 · SLe 0. Δ > 0 in both years: S0 7 · RM 2 · SL 0.
- The RM two are P1_A2_L1.5 (+0.101 / +0.016) and P2_A2_D1 (+0.109 / +0.005).
- Both fail G1 2026 (P 0.60 / 0.52), G5 (2025 top-50 −0.13) and G2 (master −0.43 / −0.50).
- Haircut: not applicable. For scale, the best 2026 Δ (+0.016) is under +0.01 after a 30–50 % haircut, and below zero under live slip.

## Parity (every headline two ways)

- **Every cell, two independent ways:**
  - Under zero slip and the resting-limit ruler, the vectorized line-array walker and an independent event-by-event loop give identical results on every fill. This is asserted on 1,472 fills + EPIC, and 0 assertions failed.
- **Today's exit re-priced and asserted equal to three references:**
  - `FX.evaluate` (S0).
  - `W.cell_rm` (resting-limit ruler).
  - The late re-study's rows (`base_pct` / `rm_base_pct`).
- **The lower-TP family (P4)** is asserted equal to `FX.evaluate` with the lower TP (S0), and to `W.cell_rm` / `W.cell_seg` with the late TP at T = 0 (RM).
- **Exit-counterfactual rule.** Every fill that never reaches the rule's trigger while today's exit is open shows Δ = 0. This is asserted in the walk and re-checked per block, with 0 violations.
  - Live-stopped read: across all 33 cells, no today-stopped fill ends deeper (saved / deeper / same is listed per table). The protection only raises the stop, so its whole cost is on the KILLED side, and that is shown separately.
- **Walker today vs the replay's recorded pct_new:** 2025 99.4 % (ticks 100 %), 2026 90.4 % (the known 1 Hz-monitor gap; the tick study saw 90.1 %), master 20 / 20.
- **Locks vs the tick study's `C.walk_lock` (lat 0):** 98.9–100 % identical.
  - The differences are 1m bars that open above the lock and trade through −3 after the arm. `walk_lock` books −3 there, and this study books the lock line.

## Earlier LITE work this cites (not re-run)

- **TICK_STUDY_MAKER_LOCK.** Locks at +1.5 / +2 → 0 / +0.5 were −0.05 to −0.14 in 2026. Here, P1_A2_L0.5 (the shared corner, re-priced at zero latency) is −0.071 in 2026, which is consistent.
- **LITE_LATE_TP_RESTUDY.** Trades at +1…+2 still reach +3 71–76 %. Here the same fact sharpens near the target: 84 % (2026) and 77 % (2025) of trades reaching +2 fill +3, and 93 % / 86 % of those reaching +2.5.
- **LITE_FADE.** 52–54 % of trades flagged by a fade signal still recover to +3, and none of the fade rules is positive in 2026 under live slip.

## Could NOT be tested

- **Live.** EPIC and DUSK are not in any export yet. They were priced on fetched 1m bars, with entry = the 5m close + 5 s.
  - DUSK's 1m bars never touch −3 (its live stop fired on an unseen print), so its replica is open. It is reported as Δ 0 because its 1m peak before the stop was only +1.24, below every arm level.
  - DEEP has no path (it is described in the live table).
- **2025 non-top-50 (564 fills) are priced on 1m bars only.** That is where all of the 2025 gains of the protection rules live. Tick-priced trades disagree. Intrabar order on 1m bars is unknown: the stop is assumed first, and an arm acts from the next bar.
- **Queue position** of a resting partial or lower TP: RM assumes ≥ 1 tick through.
- **Stop mechanics:** a lock is priced as a stop-market at the print, with live slip −0.20. A resting exchange stop could differ.
- **Slot effects** (an earlier exit frees one of LITE's 2 slots) were not modelled.

## Exact commands

```
venv/bin/python scripts/study_lite_protect_prereg.py          # freeze grid + rulers + gates (refuses overwrite) → reports/LITE_PROTECT_PREREG.txt
venv/bin/python scripts/study_lite_protect_cells.py --jobs 9  # 33 cells × (S0, RM) two ways + SL/SLp/SLe + asserts → reports/LITE_PROTECT_rows.csv, _live_rows.csv (~2 min)
venv/bin/python scripts/study_lite_protect_report.py          # gates, EPIC-problem size, two-sided tables, operator section, regime read, live → reports/LITE_PROTECT_tables.md, _gates.csv, _regime_rows.csv
```
Shared code: `scripts/study_lite_protect_common.py` (reuses study_lite_late_common, study_tick_maker_lock_common, scout_frenzy_willy_exit).

---
# LITE protect study — tables (generated by scripts/study_lite_protect_report.py)

Δ = cell − today (+3 resting / −3 / 720) on the same fill, net %/fill, both under the same ruler. S0 = zero slip (prints) · RM = resting-limit for every TP-type exit (+3, lower TP, T1 partial; ≥ 1 tick through, maker fee) · SL = RM + live slip (lock / trail / BE stop and cap −0.20; −3 stop 0.00) · SLp / SLe = −3 stop +0.20 / −0.43 (sensitivity). P = day-clustered bootstrap P(mean Δ > 0), 4,000 draws, seed 7. reached = fills on which the cell's trigger level is reached while today's exit is open (every other fill Δ = 0, asserted). SAVED / KILLED / DEEPER = today STOP-CAP improved / today TP booked less / today STOP-CAP worsened (n · Σ pts). live-stopped = today's −3 STOP fills only: saved / deeper / unchanged.

## Parity

- 2025: walker today vs the replay's recorded pct_new (same kind ∧ |Δ| < 0.10): **99.4%** of 828 (tick paths 100.0%); avg walker -0.156 vs recorded -0.142.
- 2026: walker today vs the replay's recorded pct_new (same kind ∧ |Δ| < 0.10): **90.4%** of 624 (tick paths 90.4%); avg walker +0.239 vs recorded +0.190.
- MASTER: walker vs recorded (|Δ| < 0.10): 100% of 20.
- P1 vs the tick study's C.walk_lock (lat 0, S0): P1_A2_L0.5 99.93% · P1_A2_L1 100.00% · P1_A2_L1.5 99.59% · P1_A2.25_L0.5 99.93% · P1_A2.25_L1 100.00% · P1_A2.25_L1.5 99.80% · P1_A2.5_L0.5 99.93% · P1_A2.5_L1 100.00% · P1_A2.5_L1.5 99.93% · P1_A2_L2 98.85% · P1_A2_L-1 99.80% · P1_A2_L-1.5 99.66% · P1_A2_L-2 99.46% · P1_A1.5_L1 99.86% · P1_A1.5_L1.5 99.52% · P1_A1.5_L-1 99.80% · P1_A1.5_L-1.5 99.66% · P1_A1.5_L-2 99.46% (differences = 1m bars opening above L that trade through −3 after the arm: walk_lock books −3, this study the lock line).
- Asserted per fill in study_lite_protect_cells.py (1,472 fills + EPIC): every cell way 1 (vectorized) == way 2 (event loop) under S0 and RM; today == FX.evaluate (S0), == W.cell_rm (RM), == the late-study rows; P4 == FX.evaluate(lower TP) and == W.cell_rm / W.cell_seg late-TP-at-T=0 (RM); exit-CF: never-reached fills Δ = 0 (re-checked here per block: 0 violations).


## How big is the "EPIC problem"? (today's exit, cohort fills)

peak = the highest net % reached BEFORE today's exit event (walker path). 'Reached +X, no +3' = peak ≥ +X and today's exit is not the +3 TP. Cost per fill = Σ(today's P&L of those −3 stops) ÷ ALL fills in the block (what the whole sleeve gives up to them vs a 0 exit) — and, for scale, the same vs exiting at +1. '+3 share' = of every fill that reached +X, the share that went on to fill +3.

### Regime read (hypothesis only) — regime-dependent cells (one year > 0, the other < 0, under S0 or RM): 22

Joined 1452 / 1452 BT fills to their entry stamps (25 vars, ≥ 90 % coverage). Sign granularity (signed vars 0; RSI 50; ADX 25; bull / bear / above72 % 50; volume ratios 1; else pooled median). Day units: each (day, side) one observation; Δ = mean of day means. Flag = the same side > 0 and the other ≤ 0 in BOTH years, ≥ 8 days per side per year.

1100 split tests (22 cells × 2 rulers × 25 vars). Flagged: **84**. Day-shuffled null (40 shuffles of Δ across days within each year): mean 108.5, p95 215, max 216 → share of null ≥ observed **0.62**.

# Operator's stop move after +2 %

"Once the trade reaches +X %, move the stop to 2 % / 1.5 % / 1 % max" — both readings. P1+ (profit lock): after peak ≥ +X the stop moves to +L. P1− (tighter loss): after peak ≥ +X the stop moves from −3 to −L. X = +2.0 and +1.5. (A2 L+1 and A2 L+1.5 are the same cells as in the P1 grid.)

### Regime read (hypothesis only) — regime-dependent cells (one year > 0, the other < 0, under S0 or RM): 9

Joined 1452 / 1452 BT fills to their entry stamps (25 vars, ≥ 90 % coverage). Sign granularity (signed vars 0; RSI 50; ADX 25; bull / bear / above72 % 50; volume ratios 1; else pooled median). Day units: each (day, side) one observation; Δ = mean of day means. Flag = the same side > 0 and the other ≤ 0 in BOTH years, ≥ 8 days per side per year.

450 split tests (9 cells × 2 rulers × 25 vars). Flagged: **46**. Day-shuffled null (40 shuffles of Δ across days within each year): mean 43.7, p95 94, max 107 → share of null ≥ observed **0.38**.

## ① LIVE — EPIC, DUSK (fetched 1m bars) and DEEP (recorded)

Entry = the 5m close + 5 s (not in any export yet). 1m walker: a bar touching a line books it (or the bar open when it opens through); live fills can differ (EPIC's live stop booked −3.43, 0.43 beyond the line).

- **EPIC**: 1m peak before the exit +2.60 at 15:11 UTC (live peak +2.60 at 15:11), 1m replica today: STOP -3.00 at 80 min · live booked −3.43.
- **DUSK**: live stop at 15:20 (booked −2.54). The 1m bars never touch −3 (low −2.98) and the cap window is not covered, so the 1m replica is open (UNRES). Its 1m peak before the live stop was +1.24 (verified on the fetched 1m bars) → it never reaches +1.5 / +2.0, so **no cell arms before its stop: every cell leaves DUSK at −2.54 (Δ 0)** — the stop-move rules cannot help a trade that never went up.
- **DEEP** (10-14, TP +2.80 recorded at 12 min, no path in the cache): reached +3 → P4 books its lower TP (Δ = TP − 2.80 ≈ −0.3…−0.8); P3 sells f at T1 (Δ = f × (T1 − 3)); P1 / P2 = same unless it dipped back through the lock between the arm and +3 (path unknown).
